7	**Title:** "Raw-Routed Mixture of Adapters for Time Series Foundation Models"
8	
9	## Narrative
10	
11	We prove a general principle (normalization destroys routing MI, Prop 2) → confirm via 7 converging controls including Timer-XL opposite-trajectory causal proof → validate at ρ=-0.88 across 9 datasets → propose RR-MoA as the minimal fix. Residual-IA+ matches DLinear on 62/72 cells.
12	
13	## 3 Contributions
14	
15	1. **General MI principle for any normalized backbone** (Prop 2): instance norm destroys routing MI. Predicts a priori which backbone-dataset pairs benefit via R(D). Validated ρ=-0.88 across 9 datasets. Generalizes across 5 backbones without retraining.
16	2. **7 converging causal controls**: Timer-XL opposite trajectories (1.49→1.60 vs 0.60→0.00), RevIN ablation, Moirai/Chronos negative controls, 720-run rescue sweep. AdaMix-Raw confirms diagnosis is architecture-agnostic.

13	- Overall: **62/72** across 5 backbones

Four fixes from 1,025-run diagnostic sweep: (i) residual formulation, (ii) gate-init b=-2, (iii) 5-epoch warmup, (iv) shared NLinear matching DLinear's 49K param budget.

> AGENT

Here's my honest assessment, updated for where the paper stands today (Apr 19):

---

## Estimated acceptance probability: **55-65%**

This is a "coin-flip-plus" paper — competitive but not safe. Here's the breakdown:

### Strengths (what earns acceptance)

1. **Thorough empirical work.** 4,259+ runs across 6 datasets, 5 backbones, with proper significance testing. This is substantially more thorough than a typical NeurIPS submission.
2. **Clean diagnosis + fix narrative.** The normalization→collapse→raw-routing chain is coherent and well-supported by 7 converging controls (Timer-XL opposite trajectories, RevIN ablation, Moirai/Chronos negative controls, 720-run rescue sweep, AdaMix-Raw).
3. **R(D) prediction.** ρ=-0.88 across 9 datasets is a strong empirical result — it moves beyond "we tried X and it works" to a predictive framework.
4. **Honest reporting.** 54/54 wins but also 10/72 DLinear losses, Traffic as boundary case, no overclaiming. NeurIPS reviewers appreciate this.
5. **Self-verification system.** 63 automated checks is unusual and impressive.

### Weaknesses (what risks rejection)

1. **"The fix is just routing on raw input."** Despite Timer-XL causal proof and rescue sweep, a skeptical reviewer can still argue the intellectual contribution is thin. The diagnosis is more interesting than the solution, and reviewers weight novelty heavily.

2. **DLinear gap remains partially open.** 62/72 is good but not 72/72. A reviewer who fixates on "frozen MOMENT + RR-MoA loses to a linear model on 10 cells" can frame this as undermining the TSFM motivation. The R(D) reframing helps but doesn't eliminate this attack surface.

3. **Proposition 2 isn't a tight bound.** It's a chain-rule observation with dataset-dependent leakage. A theory-leaning reviewer (R4 in your simulation) may score this 4-5 on rigor/novelty, dragging the average.

4. **37-page appendix.** Even though NeurIPS allows unlimited appendix, the sheer volume can signal "kitchen sink" — reviewers may wonder if the core contribution can't stand on its own 9 pages.

### Realistic reviewer score distribution

| Reviewer | Profile | Expected score | Risk scenario |
|---|---|:---:|---|
| R1 (TSFM specialist) | 6-7 | Appreciates depth, may worry about novelty | 5 if "obvious fix" lands |
| R2 (MoE specialist) | 6-7 | Rescue sweep is compelling | 5 if they want tighter theory |
| R3 (TS generalist) | 6-7 | Scale impresses | Solid floor |
| R4 (Theory-leaning) | 5-6 | Weakest link — Prop 2 isn't tight | 4 if strictly novelty-focused |

**Average: ~6.25.** NeurIPS typical accept threshold is ~6.0, but with high variance across ACs.

### What would move the needle

- **Upward (+5-10%):** Cross-backbone expansion landing at 80+/120 RIA+ wins; tight theoretical bound replacing Prop 2; a compelling "practitioner deploys this" case study.
- **Downward (-5-10%):** R4 scoring 4 on novelty and the AC weighting it; a reviewer finding a bug in the entropy calculations; the DLinear gap framing not landing.

### Bottom line

This is a **solid borderline-accept paper**. The diagnosis is genuinely interesting, the experiments are thorough, and the reporting is honest. The main risk is that the *solution* (route on raw input) feels too simple relative to the diagnostic machinery. NeurIPS accepts papers on a 20-25% rate; being at 55-65% means you're in the top third of submissions but not in the "obvious accept" tier (which would be 75%+).

The cross-backbone expansion results from RACE could push you toward the upper end if they're strong. Final writing polish matters at this score range — at the borderline, presentation quality and how well you preempt objections can swing individual reviewer scores by ±1.

1	"""Raw-Routed Mixture of Adapters (RR-MoA).
2	
3	Fixes the AdaMix/MoE failure: standard routers collapse because the TSFM
4	normalization cascade (RevIN + LayerNorm) destroys hidden-state heterogeneity.
5	RR-MoA routes on the RAW input instead, preserving the spectral and
6	statistical diversity needed for per-sample adapter selection.
7	
8	Key insight: the backbone processes deep semantics, while a lightweight
9	router reads the raw signal to select the adapter topology per-sample.
10	
11	Usage:
12	    python scripts/run_rr_moa.py --dataset ETTh1
13	    python scripts/run_rr_moa.py --dataset ETTh1 --top-k 2 --unfreeze frozen
14	"""
15	
16	import argparse
17	import json
18	import os
19	import sys
20	import time
21	
22	sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
23	
24	import numpy as np
25	import torch
26	import torch.nn as nn
27	import torch.nn.functional as F
28	from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset
29	
30	from feasibility.model import (
31	    load_backbone, _get_encoder_blocks, _get_hidden_dim,
32	    _disable_gradient_checkpointing,
33	)
34	from feasibility.finetune import _extract_features_batch
35	from feasibility.code_evolution import SEED_ADAPTERS, validate_adapter_code
36	from scripts.run_standard_evolution import (
37	    load_standard_data, train_adapter, _detect_backbone_type,
38	    compute_denorm_mse,
39	)
40	from feasibility.rrmoa_macro_experts import (
41	    MACRO_EXPERT_CLASSES, MACRO_EXPERT_NAMES,
42	)
43	
44	
45	# --- Expert adapter heads (same as AdaMix) ---
46	
47	class MeanPoolHead(nn.Module):
48	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, hidden=64):
49	        super().__init__()
50	        self.net = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d_model, hidden), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(hidden, output_dim))
51	    def forward(self, h):
52	        return self.net(h.mean(dim=1))
53	
54	class LastTokenHead(nn.Module):
55	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, hidden=64):
56	        super().__init__()
57	        self.net = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d_model, hidden), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(hidden, output_dim))
58	    def forward(self, h):
59	        return self.net(h[:, -1, :])
60	
61	class MaxPoolHead(nn.Module):
62	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, hidden=64):
63	        super().__init__()
64	        self.net = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d_model, hidden), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(hidden, output_dim))
65	    def forward(self, h):
66	        return self.net(h.max(dim=1).values)
67	
68	class AttentionPoolHead(nn.Module):
69	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, hidden=64):
70	        super().__init__()
71	        self.attn = nn.Linear(d_model, 1)
72	        self.net = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d_model, hidden), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(hidden, output_dim))
73	    def forward(self, h):
74	        w = torch.softmax(self.attn(h), dim=1)
75	        return self.net((h * w).sum(dim=1))
76	
77	class Conv1dPoolHead(nn.Module):
78	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, hidden=64):
79	        super().__init__()
80	        self.conv = nn.Conv1d(d_model, hidden, kernel_size=8, stride=4, padding=2)
81	        self.out = nn.Linear(hidden, output_dim)
82	    def forward(self, h):
83	        x = F.gelu(self.conv(h.permute(0, 2, 1)))
84	        return self.out(x.mean(dim=2))
85	
86	HEAD_CLASSES = [MeanPoolHead, LastTokenHead, MaxPoolHead, AttentionPoolHead, Conv1dPoolHead]
87	HEAD_NAMES = ["mean", "last", "max", "attention", "conv1d"]
88	
89	# T3.A: selectable expert pool. ``canonical`` = 5 simple pooling heads
90	# (current RR-MoA default); ``macro`` = 5 AAS-discovered cross-domain
91	# motifs from feasibility/rrmoa_macro_experts.py that unify the AAS and
92	# RR-MoA contributions (W1).
93	EXPERT_POOLS = {
94	    "canonical": (HEAD_CLASSES, HEAD_NAMES),
95	    "macro":     (MACRO_EXPERT_CLASSES, MACRO_EXPERT_NAMES),
96	    # Diversity ablation: 5 identical experts (different init, same architecture)
97	    "identical-mean":   ([MeanPoolHead],       ["mean_%d" % i for i in range(5)]),
98	    "identical-conv1d": ([Conv1dPoolHead],      ["conv1d_%d" % i for i in range(5)]),
99	    "identical-attn":   ([AttentionPoolHead],   ["attn_%d" % i for i in range(5)]),
100	}
101	
102	
103	class RawRoutedMoA(nn.Module):
104	    """Raw-Routed Mixture of Adapters.
105	
106	    Routes on RAW input (not hidden states) to avoid normalization cascade
107	    collapse (RevIN + LayerNorm). The router sees the original time series
108	    signal; the adapters see the backbone's hidden states.
109	
110	    Supports Top-K sparse routing: only the top_k experts with highest
111	    routing probability execute per sample (top_k=K gives dense mode).
112	    """
113	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, input_len=512, K=5, hidden=64, top_k=None,
114	                 router_input_mode="raw", expert_pool="canonical", entropy_reg_coef=0.0,
115	                 router_temp=1.0, router_arch="conv", expert_dropout=0.0,
116	                 rdgf=False, saib_coef=0.0):
117	        super().__init__()
118	        self.K = K
119	        self.top_k = top_k if top_k is not None else K  # default: dense
120	        self.router_temp = router_temp
121	        self.router_arch = router_arch
122	        self.expert_dropout = expert_dropout
123	        self.rdgf = rdgf
124	        self.output_dim = output_dim
125	        assert router_input_mode in ("raw", "revin", "uniform"), router_input_mode
126	        self.router_input_mode = router_input_mode
127	        self.entropy_reg_coef = entropy_reg_coef
128	        assert expert_pool in EXPERT_POOLS, expert_pool
129	        self.expert_pool = expert_pool
130	
131	        pool_classes, pool_names = EXPERT_POOLS[expert_pool]
132	        self._expert_names = pool_names
133	        # K expert adapter heads (operate on hidden states)
134	        self.adapters = nn.ModuleList([
135	            pool_classes[i % len(pool_classes)](d_model, output_dim, hidden)
136	            for i in range(K)
137	        ])
138	
139	        # RAW-INPUT router (operates on unnormalized time series)
140	        if router_arch == "ssr":
141	            # Sufficient Statistic Router: route on ONLY [μ, σ] — Prop 2 empirical proof
142	            self.router_head = nn.Linear(2, K)
143	        elif router_arch == "stats":
144	            # Stats router: route on hand-crafted [mean, std, range, slope, autocorr]
145	            self.router_head = nn.Linear(5, K)
146	        elif router_arch == "multiscale":
147	            # Multi-scale router: parallel Conv1d at k=4/16/64
148	            self.router_ms_4 = nn.Conv1d(1, 8, kernel_size=4, stride=4)
149	            self.router_ms_16 = nn.Conv1d(1, 8, kernel_size=16, stride=8)
150	            self.router_ms_64 = nn.Conv1d(1, 8, kernel_size=64, stride=32)
151	            self.router_head = nn.Linear(24, K)  # 3 scales × 8 channels pooled to 1
152	        elif router_arch == "fft":
153	            # Spectral-Temporal Router: parallel time + frequency branches
154	            self.router_time = nn.Sequential(
155	                nn.Conv1d(1, 16, kernel_size=32, stride=16, padding=8),
156	                nn.GELU(),
157	                nn.AdaptiveAvgPool1d(4),  # (B, 16, 4)
158	            )
159	            self.router_fft_proj = nn.Linear(32, 32)
160	            self.router_head = nn.Linear(96, K)  # 64 time + 32 spectral
161	        elif router_arch == "linear":
162	            # Naive linear router: single Linear(input_len, K), no temporal conv
163	            # V1 ablation: tests whether raw signal alone suffices without
164	            # temporal feature extraction
165	            self.router_head = nn.Linear(input_len, K)
166	        else:
167	            # Default conv router
168	            self.router = nn.Sequential(
169	                nn.Conv1d(1, 16, kernel_size=32, stride=16, padding=8),
170	                nn.GELU(),
171	                nn.AdaptiveAvgPool1d(4),  # (B, 16, 4)
172	            )
173	            self.router_head = nn.Linear(64, K)  # 16*4 = 64
174	
175	        self.load_balance_coeff = 0.01
176	
177	        # SAIB: Statistic-Aware Information Bottleneck auxiliary loss
178	        self.saib_coef = saib_coef
179	        if saib_coef > 0 and router_arch in ("conv", "fft"):
180	            self.saib_head = nn.Linear(64, 2)  # predict [mu, sigma] from router latent
181	
182	    def _compute_logits(self, raw_input):
183	        if self.router_input_mode == "uniform":
184	            # T1.B ensemble-vs-specialization control: force a constant
185	            # uniform mixture by returning zero logits (softmax -> 1/K).
186	            # The router's Conv1d/Linear params are still present and get
187	            # zero gradient; this keeps the rest of the training loop (and
188	            # the checkpointable param set) identical to the raw / revin
189	            # variants so any MSE gap is attributable to the routing
190	            # decision alone, not to capacity differences.
191	            B = raw_input.shape[0]
192	            return torch.zeros(B, self.K, device=raw_input.device, dtype=raw_input.dtype)
193	        if self.router_input_mode == "revin":
194	            # Per-window RevIN-style normalization: zero mean, unit variance
195	            # along the temporal dimension. This replicates what MOMENT's
196	            # internal RevIN would do if the router saw post-normalization
197	            # data, and strips per-window trend/amplitude/volatility. The
198	            # ablation tests whether routing gains come from rawness of the
199	            # signal or merely from bypassing the hidden states.
200	            mu = raw_input.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
201	            sigma = raw_input.std(dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-5
202	            x = (raw_input - mu) / sigma
203	        else:
204	            x = raw_input
205	
206	        if self.router_arch == "ssr":
207	            # Sufficient Statistic Router: just [μ, σ] — 2 scalars, ~10 params
208	            mu = x.mean(dim=-1)
209	            sigma = x.std(dim=-1)
210	            return self.router_head(torch.stack([mu, sigma], dim=-1))
211	
212	        if self.router_arch == "stats":
213	            # Hand-crafted statistics: [mean, std, range, slope, autocorr_lag1]
214	            mu = x.mean(dim=-1)
215	            sigma = x.std(dim=-1)
216	            rng = x.max(dim=-1).values - x.min(dim=-1).values
217	            # Linear regression slope
218	            t = torch.arange(x.shape[-1], dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device)
219	            t_centered = t - t.mean()
220	            slope = ((x - mu.unsqueeze(-1)) * t_centered).sum(dim=-1) / (t_centered ** 2).sum()
221	            # Autocorrelation at lag 1
222	            x_centered = x - mu.unsqueeze(-1)
223	            autocorr = (x_centered[:, :-1] * x_centered[:, 1:]).mean(dim=-1) / (sigma ** 2 + 1e-8)
224	            stats = torch.stack([mu, sigma, rng, slope, autocorr], dim=-1)  # (B, 5)
225	            return self.router_head(stats)
226	
227	        if self.router_arch == "multiscale":
228	            x1 = x.unsqueeze(1)
229	            f4 = F.adaptive_avg_pool1d(F.gelu(self.router_ms_4(x1)), 1).flatten(1)   # (B, 8)
230	            f16 = F.adaptive_avg_pool1d(F.gelu(self.router_ms_16(x1)), 1).flatten(1)  # (B, 8)
231	            f64 = F.adaptive_avg_pool1d(F.gelu(self.router_ms_64(x1)), 1).flatten(1)  # (B, 8)
232	            return self.router_head(torch.cat([f4, f16, f64], dim=-1))
233	
234	        if self.router_arch == "fft":
235	            x1 = x.unsqueeze(1)  # (B, 1, input_len)
236	            time_feat = self.router_time(x1).flatten(1)  # (B, 64)
237	            fft_amp = torch.fft.rfft(x, dim=-1).abs()  # (B, input_len//2 + 1)
238	            fft_top = fft_amp[:, 1:33]  # skip DC component, take bins 1-32
239	            spec_feat = F.gelu(self.router_fft_proj(fft_top))  # (B, 32)
240	            return self.router_head(torch.cat([time_feat, spec_feat], dim=-1))
241	
242	        if self.router_arch == "linear":
243	            return self.router_head(x)  # (B, input_len) -> (B, K) directly
244	
245	        # Default conv router
246	        x = x.unsqueeze(1)  # (B, 1, input_len)
247	        router_feat = self.router(x).flatten(1)  # (B, 64)
248	        return self.router_head(router_feat)  # (B, K)
249	
250	    def forward(self, hidden_states, raw_input):
251	        """
252	        hidden_states: (B, T, d_model) from backbone
253	        raw_input: (B, input_len) raw time series
254	        """
255	        logits = self._compute_logits(raw_input)  # (B, K)
256	        if self.router_temp != 1.0:
257	            logits = logits / self.router_temp
258	
259	        # Expert dropout during training: randomly zero out one expert's weight
260	        if self.training and self.expert_dropout > 0:
261	            drop_mask = torch.ones(logits.shape, device=logits.device)
262	            drop_idx = torch.randint(0, self.K, (logits.shape[0],), device=logits.device)
263	            if torch.rand(1).item() < self.expert_dropout:
264	                drop_mask[torch.arange(logits.shape[0]), drop_idx] = -1e9
265	                logits = logits + drop_mask
266	
267	        if self.top_k >= self.K:
268	            # Dense mode: all experts
269	            weights = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)  # (B, K)
270	            outputs = torch.stack([a(hidden_states) for a in self.adapters], dim=1)
271	            return (weights.unsqueeze(-1) * outputs).sum(dim=1)
272	
273	        # Sparse Top-K routing
274	        B = hidden_states.shape[0]
275	        topk_vals, topk_idx = logits.topk(self.top_k, dim=-1)  # (B, top_k)
276	        weights = F.softmax(topk_vals, dim=-1)  # (B, top_k) normalized over selected
277	
278	        result = torch.zeros(B, self.output_dim, device=hidden_states.device, dtype=hidden_states.dtype)
279	        for i in range(self.top_k):
280	            expert_ids = topk_idx[:, i]  # (B,)
281	            w = weights[:, i].unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, 1)
282	            for k in range(self.K):
283	                mask = (expert_ids == k)
284	                if mask.any():
285	                    result[mask] += w[mask] * self.adapters[k](hidden_states[mask])
286	
287	        return result
288	
289	    def get_routing_stats(self, raw_input):
290	        """Full softmax routing weights for analysis (always dense)."""
291	        with torch.no_grad():
292	            logits = self._compute_logits(raw_input)
293	            return F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
294	
295	    def load_balance_loss(self, raw_input):
296	        logits = self._compute_logits(raw_input)
297	        weights = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
298	        f_i = weights.mean(dim=0)
299	        p_i = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1).mean(dim=0)
300	        return self.K * (f_i * p_i).sum()
301	
302	    def param_count(self):
303	        return sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters())
304	
305	    def entropy_regularization(self, raw_input):
306	        """Negative entropy of the router distribution: -H(p).
307	        Minimizing this maximizes routing entropy (diversity)."""
308	        logits = self._compute_logits(raw_input)
309	        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
310	        H = -(probs * torch.log(probs.clamp_min(1e-10))).sum(dim=-1).mean()
311	        return -H
312	
313	    def saib_loss(self, raw_input):
314	        """SAIB: force router latent to encode window [mu, sigma].
315	        Operationalizes Proposition 2 as an explicit training objective."""
316	        if self.saib_coef <= 0 or self.router_arch not in ("conv", "fft"):
317	            return torch.tensor(0.0, device=raw_input.device)
318	        x = raw_input
319	        if self.router_input_mode == "revin":
320	            mu = x.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
321	            sigma = x.std(dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-5
322	            x = (x - mu) / sigma
323	        if self.router_arch == "fft":
324	            router_feat = self.router_time(x.unsqueeze(1)).flatten(1)  # (B, 64)
325	        else:
326	            router_feat = self.router(x.unsqueeze(1)).flatten(1)  # (B, 64)
327	        pred_stats = self.saib_head(router_feat)  # (B, 2)
328	        true_mu = raw_input.mean(dim=-1)
329	        true_sigma = raw_input.std(dim=-1)
330	        true_stats = torch.stack([true_mu, true_sigma], dim=-1)
331	        return F.mse_loss(pred_stats, true_stats)
332	
333	
334	def train_rr_moa(model, blocks, X_train, Y_train, X_test, Y_test,
335	                 device="cuda", n_epochs=15, forecast_horizon=96, batch_size=128,
336	                 backbone_type="moment", K=5, hidden=64, top_k=None,
337	                 router_input_mode="raw", test_ch=None, scaler=None,
338	                 expert_pool="canonical", entropy_reg_coef=0.0, router_temp=1.0,
339	                 router_arch="conv", expert_dropout=0.0, rdgf=False,
340	                 saib_coef=0.0, freeze_router=False):
341	    """Train RR-MoA: raw-routed mixture of adapters."""
342	    hdim = _get_hidden_dim(model)
343	    adapter = RawRoutedMoA(
344	        hdim, forecast_horizon, input_len=512, K=K, hidden=hidden, top_k=top_k,
345	        router_input_mode=router_input_mode, expert_pool=expert_pool,
346	        entropy_reg_coef=entropy_reg_coef, router_temp=router_temp,
347	        router_arch=router_arch, expert_dropout=expert_dropout,
348	        rdgf=rdgf, saib_coef=saib_coef,
349	    ).to(device)
350	
351	    # V1 ablation: freeze router at random init
352	    if freeze_router:
353	        for name, p in adapter.named_parameters():
354	            if "router" in name:
355	                p.requires_grad = False
356	
357	    trainable = list(p for p in adapter.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
358	    pids = {id(p) for p in trainable}
359	    for p in model.parameters():
360	        if p.requires_grad and id(p) not in pids:
361	            trainable.append(p)
362	            pids.add(id(p))
363	
364	    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(trainable, lr=1e-3)
365	    mse_fn = nn.MSELoss()
366	    use_amp = device == "cuda"
367	
368	    train_loader = DataLoader(TensorDataset(
369	        torch.from_numpy(X_train).float(), torch.from_numpy(Y_train).float(),
370	    ), batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True)
371	
372	    for epoch in range(n_epochs):
373	        model.train(); adapter.train()
374	        for bx, by in train_loader:
375	            bx_raw = bx.to(device)  # (B, 512) raw input
376	            bx_enc = bx.to(device).unsqueeze(1)  # (B, 1, 512) for backbone
377	            by = by.to(device)
378	            mask = torch.ones(bx_enc.shape[0], bx_enc.shape[2], device=device)
379	
380	            with torch.amp.autocast('cuda', dtype=torch.bfloat16, enabled=use_amp):
381	                feat = _extract_features_batch(model, blocks, bx_enc, mask, backbone_type=backbone_type)
382	                if rdgf:
383	                    # RDGF: main loss uses detached H (no backbone gradient from weighted path)
384	                    pred = adapter(feat.detach(), bx_raw)
385	                    loss = mse_fn(pred, by)
386	                    # Auxiliary: uniform-weighted experts on live H (backbone gets uniform gradient)
387	                    outputs_live = torch.stack([a(feat) for a in adapter.adapters], dim=1)
388	                    aux_pred = outputs_live.mean(dim=1)
389	                    loss = loss + mse_fn(aux_pred, by)
390	                else:
391	                    pred = adapter(feat, bx_raw)
392	                    loss = mse_fn(pred, by)
393	                loss = loss + adapter.load_balance_coeff * adapter.load_balance_loss(bx_raw)
394	                if adapter.entropy_reg_coef > 0:
395	                    loss = loss + adapter.entropy_reg_coef * adapter.entropy_regularization(bx_raw)
396	                if adapter.saib_coef > 0:
397	                    loss = loss + adapter.saib_coef * adapter.saib_loss(bx_raw)
398	
399	            optimizer.zero_grad()
400	            loss.backward()
401	            optimizer.step()
402	
403	    # Evaluate
404	    model.eval(); adapter.eval()
405	    test_loader = DataLoader(TensorDataset(
406	        torch.from_numpy(X_test).float(), torch.from_numpy(Y_test).float(),
407	    ), batch_size=batch_size)
408	
409	    preds, tgts = [], []
410	    all_routing = []
411	    with torch.no_grad(), torch.amp.autocast('cuda', dtype=torch.bfloat16, enabled=use_amp):
412	        for bx, by in test_loader:
413	            bx_raw = bx.to(device)
414	            bx_enc = bx.to(device).unsqueeze(1)
415	            by = by.to(device)
416	            mask = torch.ones(bx_enc.shape[0], bx_enc.shape[2], device=device)
417	            feat = _extract_features_batch(model, blocks, bx_enc, mask, backbone_type=backbone_type)
418	            preds.append(adapter(feat, bx_raw).float().cpu())
419	            tgts.append(by.cpu())
420	            all_routing.append(adapter.get_routing_stats(bx_raw).cpu())
421	
422	    preds, tgts = torch.cat(preds), torch.cat(tgts)
423	    routing = torch.cat(all_routing)
424	    mse = nn.MSELoss()(preds, tgts).item()
425	    mae = nn.L1Loss()(preds, tgts).item()
426	
427	    mean_routing = routing.mean(dim=0).tolist()
428	    routing_entropy = -(routing * torch.log(routing + 1e-10)).sum(dim=-1).mean().item()
429	    routing_max = routing.max(dim=-1).values.mean().item()
430	
431	    # T1.B analysis support: per-sample routing-weight variance across the
432	    # test set. A near-zero value indicates the router is effectively
433	    # constant, in which case RR-MoA is operating as a soft ensemble rather
434	    # than a per-sample mixture of specialists.
435	    routing_np = routing.float().numpy()
436	    per_sample_std = float(np.mean(np.std(routing_np, axis=1)))
437	    cross_sample_var = float(np.mean(np.var(routing_np, axis=0)))
438	
439	    names = adapter._expert_names
440	    out = {
441	        "mse": mse, "mae": mae, "param_count": adapter.param_count(),
442	        "top_k": adapter.top_k,
443	        "router_input_mode": adapter.router_input_mode,
444	        "expert_pool": adapter.expert_pool,
445	        "routing": {names[i]: round(w, 3) for i, w in enumerate(mean_routing[:len(names)])},
446	        "routing_entropy": routing_entropy,
447	        "routing_max_weight": routing_max,
448	        "routing_per_sample_std": per_sample_std,
449	        "routing_cross_sample_var": cross_sample_var,
450	    }
451	
452	    # T1.A denormalized MSE in original (un-standardized) units.
453	    if test_ch is not None and scaler is not None:
454	        mse_d, mae_d = compute_denorm_mse(preds, tgts, test_ch, scaler)
455	        out["mse_denorm"] = mse_d
456	        out["mae_denorm"] = mae_d
457	
458	    return out
459	
460	
461	def _apply_unfreeze(blocks, unfreeze):
462	    """Selectively unfreeze encoder blocks."""
463	    n = len(blocks)
464	    if unfreeze == "frozen":
465	        return  # all frozen
466	    elif unfreeze == "last2":
467	        start = max(0, n - 2)
468	    elif unfreeze == "last4":
469	        start = max(0, n - 4)
470	    elif unfreeze == "all":
471	        start = 0
472	    else:
473	        raise ValueError("Unknown unfreeze: %s" % unfreeze)
474	    for i in range(start, n):
475	        for p in blocks[i].parameters():
476	            p.requires_grad = True
477	
478	
479	def main():
480	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
481	    parser.add_argument("--dataset", default="ETTh1")
482	    parser.add_argument("--horizon", type=int, default=96)
483	    parser.add_argument("--K", type=int, default=5)
484	    parser.add_argument("--top-k", type=int, default=None,
485	                        help="Top-K sparse routing (default: dense, all K experts)")
486	    parser.add_argument("--router-input-mode", default="raw",
487	                        choices=["raw", "revin", "uniform"],
488	                        help="Signal the gate reads: raw (channel-standardized input, default); "
489	                             "revin (per-window zero-mean unit-variance, rawness-vs-bypass "
490	                             "ablation); uniform (no routing, fixed 1/K weights, "
491	                             "ensemble-vs-specialization control).")
492	    parser.add_argument("--unfreeze", default="last4", choices=["frozen", "last2", "last4", "all"],
493	                        help="Backbone unfreezing strategy")
494	    parser.add_argument("--expert-pool", default="canonical",
495	                        choices=list(EXPERT_POOLS.keys()),
496	                        help="Which expert pool to populate RR-MoA with. "
497	                             "'canonical' = 5 simple pooling heads (mean/last/max/attn/conv1d, "
498	                             "current default). 'macro' = 5 AAS-distilled cross-domain motifs "
499	                             "from feasibility/rrmoa_macro_experts.py (BN+mean, multi-scale "
500	                             "conv, Conv1d+BN+residual, depthwise separable, gated conv). "
501	                             "The 'macro' option is the T3.A integration experiment that "
502	                             "unifies the AAS and RR-MoA contributions (reviewer W1).")
503	    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
504	    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=15)
505	    parser.add_argument("--backbone", default="AutonLab/MOMENT-1-small")
506	    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=128,
507	                        help="Training/eval batch size (reduce for large backbones)")
508	    parser.add_argument("--device", default="cuda")
509	    parser.add_argument("--no-baselines", action="store_true",
510	                        help="Skip baseline evaluation (faster for ablation sweeps)")
511	    parser.add_argument("--disable-revin", action="store_true",
512	                        help="Controlled ablation: disable RevIN inside MOMENT backbone")
513	    parser.add_argument("--entropy-reg", type=float, default=0.0,
514	                        help="Entropy reg coefficient for the raw router (B9 symmetry test)")
515	    parser.add_argument("--router-temp", type=float, default=1.0,
516	                        help="Router softmax temperature (>1 softer, <1 sharper)")
517	    parser.add_argument("--router-arch", default="conv",
518	                        choices=["conv", "stats", "multiscale", "ssr", "fft", "linear"],
519	                        help="Router architecture: conv (default), stats (hand-crafted), multiscale, fft (spectral-temporal), linear (naive V1 ablation)")
520	    parser.add_argument("--freeze-router", action="store_true",
521	                        help="Freeze router weights at random init (V1 ablation: tests whether router must learn)")
522	    parser.add_argument("--saib-coef", type=float, default=0.0,
523	                        help="SAIB auxiliary loss coefficient (0 = disabled). Forces router latent to encode window mu/sigma.")
524	    parser.add_argument("--expert-dropout", type=float, default=0.0,
525	                        help="Expert dropout probability during training")
526	    parser.add_argument("--rdgf", action="store_true",
527	                        help="Router-Detached Gradient Flow: unfreeze backbone with uniform gradient")
528	    args = parser.parse_args()
529	
530	    os.makedirs("results/rr_moa", exist_ok=True)
531	    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
532	    np.random.seed(args.seed)
533	
534	    model = load_backbone(args.backbone, args.device,
535	                          disable_revin=args.disable_revin)
536	    _disable_gradient_checkpointing(model)
537	    blocks = _get_encoder_blocks(model)
538	    hdim = _get_hidden_dim(model)
539	    bb_type = _detect_backbone_type(args.backbone)
540	
541	    for p in model.parameters():
542	        p.requires_grad = False
543	    _apply_unfreeze(blocks, args.unfreeze)
544	
545	    n_unfrozen = sum(1 for b in blocks for p in b.parameters() if p.requires_grad) > 0
546	    backbone_trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
547	    print("Unfreeze=%s, backbone trainable params=%d" % (args.unfreeze, backbone_trainable))
548	
549	    splits, _ = load_standard_data(args.dataset, args.horizon)
550	    X_train, Y_train = splits["train"]
551	    X_test, Y_test = splits["test"]
552	    test_ch = splits.get("test_ch")
553	    scaler = splits.get("_scaler")
554	    print("%s H=%d: train=%d, test=%d" % (args.dataset, args.horizon, len(X_train), len(X_test)))
555	
556	    # === RR-MoA ===
557	    top_k_label = "top%d" % args.top_k if args.top_k else "dense"
558	    print("\nRR-MoA: %s K=%d %s unfreeze=%s seed=%d router_input=%s" % (
559	        args.dataset, args.K, top_k_label, args.unfreeze, args.seed, args.router_input_mode))
560	    start = time.time()
561	    result = train_rr_moa(model, blocks, X_train, Y_train, X_test, Y_test,
562	                          device=args.device, forecast_horizon=args.horizon,
563	                          backbone_type=bb_type, K=args.K, top_k=args.top_k,
564	                          n_epochs=args.epochs, router_input_mode=args.router_input_mode,
565	                          test_ch=test_ch, scaler=scaler,
566	                          expert_pool=args.expert_pool,
567	                          batch_size=args.batch_size,
568	                          entropy_reg_coef=args.entropy_reg,
569	                          router_temp=args.router_temp,
570	                          router_arch=args.router_arch,
571	                          expert_dropout=args.expert_dropout,
572	                          rdgf=args.rdgf,
573	                          saib_coef=args.saib_coef,
574	                          freeze_router=args.freeze_router)
575	    elapsed = time.time() - start
576	
577	    print("RR-MoA: MSE=%.4f  params=%d  time=%.0fs" % (result["mse"], result["param_count"], elapsed))
578	    if "mse_denorm" in result:
579	        print("RR-MoA: MSE_denorm=%.4f  MAE_denorm=%.4f (original units)" % (
580	            result["mse_denorm"], result["mae_denorm"]))
581	    print("Routing: %s" % result["routing"])
582	    print("Routing entropy: %.3f / %.3f (max)" % (result["routing_entropy"], np.log(args.K)))
583	    print("Routing max weight: %.3f (1.0 = collapsed)" % result["routing_max_weight"])
584	    print("Routing per-sample std: %.4f  cross-sample var: %.6f" % (
585	        result["routing_per_sample_std"], result["routing_cross_sample_var"]))
586	
587	    # === Baselines ===
588	    baseline_results = {}
589	    if not args.no_baselines:
590	        print("\nBaselines (%d epochs, unfreeze=%s):" % (args.epochs, args.unfreeze))
591	        model2 = load_backbone(args.backbone, args.device,
592	                               disable_revin=args.disable_revin)
593	        _disable_gradient_checkpointing(model2)
594	        blocks2 = _get_encoder_blocks(model2)
595	        for p in model2.parameters():
596	            p.requires_grad = False
597	        _apply_unfreeze(blocks2, args.unfreeze)
598	
599	        baselines = {"linear": SEED_ADAPTERS[0], "attention": SEED_ADAPTERS[3], "conv": SEED_ADAPTERS[4]}
600	        for name, code in baselines.items():
601	            try:
602	                tr = train_adapter(code, model2, blocks2, X_train, Y_train, X_test, Y_test,
603	                                   device=args.device, n_epochs=args.epochs, forecast_horizon=args.horizon,
604	                                   backbone_type=bb_type, eval_ch=test_ch, scaler=scaler)
605	                baseline_results[name] = tr
606	                if "mse_denorm" in tr:
607	                    print("  %-15s MSE=%.4f  MSE_denorm=%.4f" % (name, tr["mse"], tr["mse_denorm"]))
608	                else:
609	                    print("  %-15s MSE=%.4f" % (name, tr["mse"]))
610	            except Exception as e:
611	                print("  %-15s ERROR: %s" % (name, e))
612	
613	    if baseline_results:
614	        best_bl = min(baseline_results.values(), key=lambda x: x["mse"])["mse"]
615	        best_bl_name = min(baseline_results, key=lambda k: baseline_results[k]["mse"])
616	        delta = (result["mse"] - best_bl) / best_bl * 100
617	        winner = "RR-MoA" if result["mse"] < best_bl else "BASELINE"
618	        print("\n>>> %s wins: RR-MoA=%.4f vs %s=%.4f  delta=%+.1f%%" % (
619	            winner, result["mse"], best_bl_name, best_bl, delta))
620	    else:
621	        winner = "N/A"
622	        delta = 0.0
623	
624	    # Record the per-channel scale vector so that verify.py / downstream
625	    # analysis can reconstruct denorm numbers without re-loading the dataset.
626	    scaler_info = None
627	    if scaler is not None:
628	        scaler_info = {
629	            "scale_": [float(x) for x in scaler.scale_],
630	            "mean_": [float(x) for x in scaler.mean_],
631	            "n_features_in_": int(getattr(scaler, "n_features_in_", len(scaler.scale_))),
632	            "mean_scale_sq": float(np.mean(scaler.scale_ ** 2)),
633	        }
634	
635	    save_data = {
636	        "dataset": args.dataset, "horizon": args.horizon, "seed": args.seed,
637	        "K": args.K, "top_k": args.top_k or args.K, "unfreeze": args.unfreeze,
638	        "router_input_mode": args.router_input_mode,
639	        "disable_revin": args.disable_revin,
640	        "backbone_trainable_params": backbone_trainable,
641	        "rr_moa": result, "elapsed": elapsed,
642	        "baselines": {k: v for k, v in baseline_results.items()},
643	        "winner": winner, "delta_pct": delta,
644	        "scaler": scaler_info,
645	    }
646	    # Append router mode / pool / backbone suffixes only for non-default
647	    # options so existing raw+canonical JSONs keep their current filenames
648	    # (and evidence_vm/verify.py's paths keep working).
649	    suffixes = []
650	    if args.disable_revin:
651	        suffixes.append("no-revin")
652	    if args.router_input_mode != "raw":
653	        suffixes.append("router-%s" % args.router_input_mode)
654	    if args.expert_pool != "canonical":
655	        suffixes.append("pool-%s" % args.expert_pool)
656	    if args.entropy_reg > 0:
657	        suffixes.append("entreg-%.3g" % args.entropy_reg)
658	    if args.router_temp != 1.0:
659	        suffixes.append("temp-%.3g" % args.router_temp)
660	    if args.router_arch != "conv":
661	        suffixes.append("rarch-%s" % args.router_arch)
662	    if args.expert_dropout > 0:
663	        suffixes.append("edrop-%.2g" % args.expert_dropout)
664	    if args.rdgf:
665	        suffixes.append("rdgf")
666	    if args.freeze_router:
667	        suffixes.append("frozenrouter")
668	    if args.saib_coef > 0:
669	        suffixes.append("saib-%.3g" % args.saib_coef)
670	    # Backbone suffix for non-default backbones
671	    bb_lower = args.backbone.lower()
672	    if "moment" in bb_lower and "large" in bb_lower:
673	        suffixes.append("bb-moment-large")
674	    elif "moirai-moe" in bb_lower or "moirai_moe" in bb_lower or "moiraimoe" in bb_lower:
675	        suffixes.append("bb-moirai-moe")
676	    elif "moirai" in bb_lower:
677	        suffixes.append("bb-moirai")
678	    elif "chronos" in bb_lower:
679	        suffixes.append("bb-chronos")
680	    elif args.backbone != "AutonLab/MOMENT-1-small":
681	        suffixes.append("bb-" + args.backbone.split("/")[-1].lower())
682	    suffix = ("_" + "_".join(suffixes)) if suffixes else ""
683	    path = "results/rr_moa/%s_H%d_K%d_%s_%s_%d%s.json" % (
684	        args.dataset, args.horizon, args.K, top_k_label, args.unfreeze,
685	        args.seed, suffix)
686	    with open(path, "w") as f:
687	        json.dump(save_data, f, indent=2, default=str)
688	    print("Saved to %s" % path)
689

692	    main()

1	"""AdaMix: Learned Adapter Mixture for TSFM Adaptation.
2	
3	Instead of searching for one adapter architecture, trains a fixed mixture
4	of K canonical adapter heads with a learned per-sample router.
5	
6	Key insight from DARTS experiments: the soft mixture (undiscretized supernet)
7	beats any single discretized architecture. AdaMix makes this permanent —
8	instance-level routing lets different time series windows get different
9	adapter combinations.
10	
11	Usage:
12	    python scripts/run_adamix.py --dataset ETTh1
13	    python scripts/run_adamix.py --dataset ETTh1 --K 3
14	"""
15	
16	import argparse
17	import json
18	import os
19	import sys
20	import time
21	
22	sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
23	
24	import numpy as np
25	import torch
26	import torch.nn as nn
27	import torch.nn.functional as F
28	from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset
29	
30	from feasibility.model import (
31	    load_backbone, _get_encoder_blocks, _get_hidden_dim,
32	    _disable_gradient_checkpointing,
33	)
34	from feasibility.finetune import _extract_features_batch
35	from feasibility.code_evolution import SEED_ADAPTERS, validate_adapter_code
36	from scripts.run_standard_evolution import (
37	    load_standard_data, train_adapter, _detect_backbone_type,
38	    compute_denorm_mse,
39	)
40	
41	
42	# --- Individual adapter heads (simple, canonical designs) ---
43	
44	class MeanPoolHead(nn.Module):
45	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, hidden=64):
46	        super().__init__()
47	        self.fc = nn.Linear(d_model, hidden)
48	        self.out = nn.Linear(hidden, output_dim)
49	
50	    def forward(self, h):
51	        x = h.mean(dim=1)
52	        return self.out(F.gelu(self.fc(x)))
53	
54	
55	class LastTokenHead(nn.Module):
56	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, hidden=64):
57	        super().__init__()
58	        self.fc = nn.Linear(d_model, hidden)
59	        self.out = nn.Linear(hidden, output_dim)
60	
61	    def forward(self, h):
62	        x = h[:, -1, :]
63	        return self.out(F.gelu(self.fc(x)))
64	
65	
66	class MaxPoolHead(nn.Module):
67	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, hidden=64):
68	        super().__init__()
69	        self.fc = nn.Linear(d_model, hidden)
70	        self.out = nn.Linear(hidden, output_dim)
71	
72	    def forward(self, h):
73	        x = h.max(dim=1).values
74	        return self.out(F.gelu(self.fc(x)))
75	
76	
77	class AttentionPoolHead(nn.Module):
78	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, hidden=64):
79	        super().__init__()
80	        self.attn = nn.Linear(d_model, 1)
81	        self.fc = nn.Linear(d_model, hidden)
82	        self.out = nn.Linear(hidden, output_dim)
83	
84	    def forward(self, h):
85	        w = torch.softmax(self.attn(h), dim=1)
86	        x = (h * w).sum(dim=1)
87	        return self.out(F.gelu(self.fc(x)))
88	
89	
90	class Conv1dPoolHead(nn.Module):
91	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, hidden=64):
92	        super().__init__()
93	        self.conv = nn.Conv1d(d_model, hidden, kernel_size=8, stride=4, padding=2)
94	        self.out = nn.Linear(hidden, output_dim)
95	
96	    def forward(self, h):
97	        x = h.permute(0, 2, 1)  # (B, d, T)
98	        x = F.gelu(self.conv(x))  # (B, hidden, T')
99	        x = x.mean(dim=2)  # (B, hidden)
100	        return self.out(x)
101	
102	
103	# --- AdaMix: Learned Adapter Mixture ---
104	
105	HEAD_CLASSES = [MeanPoolHead, LastTokenHead, MaxPoolHead, AttentionPoolHead, Conv1dPoolHead]
106	HEAD_NAMES = ["mean", "last", "max", "attention", "conv1d"]
107	
108	
109	class AdaMix(nn.Module):
110	    """Learned mixture of K canonical adapter heads with per-sample routing.
111	
112	    The router produces instance-level weights over K adapter heads.
113	    Different time series windows get different adapter combinations.
114	
115	    Supports four MoE-rescue variants evaluated in the RR-MoA rescue sub-table:
116	      - router_type="softmax": standard Switch Transformer softmax gating
117	      - router_type="relu":     ReMoE ReLU gating (Wang et al. ICLR 2025)
118	      - router_type="expert-choice": Zhou et al. 2022 per-expert top-k
119	      - load_balance_variant="mean-prob": legacy AdaMix (f_i = P_i; preserved
120	        for reproducibility of prior paper Table 2 numbers)
121	      - load_balance_variant="argmax":    correct Switch Transformer Eq. 4-6
122	        (f_i uses argmax indicator, P_i the mean softmax probability)
123	
124	    router_input="hidden" (default): routes on mean-pooled hidden states.
125	    router_input="raw": routes on raw pre-normalization input (Exp D ablation).
126	    """
127	    def __init__(self, d_model, output_dim, K=5, hidden=64, router_hidden=32,
128	                 router_type="softmax",
129	                 load_balance_coef=0.01,
130	                 load_balance_variant="mean-prob",
131	                 entropy_reg_coef=0.0,
132	                 z_loss_coef=0.0,
133	                 relu_l1_coef=0.0,
134	                 capacity_factor=2.0,
135	                 router_input="hidden",
136	                 input_len=512):
137	        super().__init__()
138	        self.K = K
139	        self.d_model = d_model
140	        self.router_type = router_type
141	        self.router_input = router_input
142	        self.load_balance_coef = load_balance_coef
143	        self.load_balance_variant = load_balance_variant
144	        self.entropy_reg_coef = entropy_reg_coef
145	        self.z_loss_coef = z_loss_coef
146	        self.relu_l1_coef = relu_l1_coef
147	        self.capacity_factor = capacity_factor
148	
149	        # Back-compat attribute (older scripts reference .load_balance_coeff)
150	        self.load_balance_coeff = load_balance_coef
151	
152	        # K adapter heads
153	        self.adapters = nn.ModuleList([
154	            HEAD_CLASSES[i % len(HEAD_CLASSES)](d_model, output_dim, hidden)
155	            for i in range(K)
156	        ])
157	
158	        # Instance-level router
159	        if router_input == "raw":
160	            # Exp D: Conv1d raw-input router (same architecture as RR-MoA)
161	            self.raw_router = nn.Sequential(
162	                nn.Conv1d(1, 16, kernel_size=32, stride=16, padding=8),
163	                nn.GELU(),
164	                nn.AdaptiveAvgPool1d(4),
165	            )
166	            self.router = nn.Linear(64, K)
167	        else:
168	            self.router = nn.Sequential(
169	                nn.Linear(d_model, router_hidden),
170	                nn.GELU(),
171	                nn.Linear(router_hidden, K),
172	            )
173	
174	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
175	    #  Routing primitives                                                #
176	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
177	
178	    def _compute_logits(self, hidden_states, raw_input=None):
179	        """Shared: mean-pool to (B, d_model) and project to (B, K).
180	        If router_input='raw', uses raw_input instead of hidden_states."""
181	        if self.router_input == "raw" and raw_input is not None:
182	            x = raw_input.unsqueeze(1)  # (B, 1, L)
183	            feat = self.raw_router(x).flatten(1)  # (B, 64)
184	            return self.router(feat)
185	        h_summary = hidden_states.mean(dim=1)
186	        return self.router(h_summary)  # (B, K)
187	
188	    def _compute_weights(self, logits):
189	        """Dispatch to the requested routing scheme.  Returns (B, K) per-sample
190	        mixture weights that sum to 1 along dim=-1 (or close to it, for
191	        expert-choice fallback samples)."""
192	        if self.router_type == "softmax":
193	            return F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
194	
195	        if self.router_type == "relu":
196	            # ReMoE: unnormalized ReLU gate.  For a mixture-of-adapters we
197	            # renormalize per sample so the output magnitude matches softmax;
198	            # L1 sparsity pressure is applied on the raw ReLU values via
199	            # relu_l1_loss() below.
200	            raw = F.relu(logits)
201	            denom = raw.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
202	            # Fallback to softmax for any sample where all logits are <=0.
203	            fallback = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
204	            weights = torch.where(
205	                denom > 1e-8,
206	                raw / denom.clamp_min(1e-8),
207	                fallback,
208	            )
209	            return weights
210	
211	        if self.router_type == "expert-choice":
212	            # Zhou et al. 2022 expert-choice: each expert picks top-k samples,
213	            # with k = B * c / K (c = capacity factor).  Simplified for the
214	            # mixture-of-adapters context where we cannot drop samples.
215	            B = logits.shape[0]
216	            S = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)           # (B, K) token->expert probs
217	            k = max(1, min(B, int(round(B * self.capacity_factor / self.K))))
218	            S_T = S.transpose(0, 1)                 # (K, B)
219	            _, top_idx = torch.topk(S_T, k, dim=-1)  # (K, k)
220	            mask = torch.zeros_like(S_T)
221	            mask.scatter_(1, top_idx, 1.0)          # (K, B) 1 where expert picked
222	            gate = S_T * mask                       # (K, B)
223	            weights = gate.transpose(0, 1)          # (B, K)
224	            denom = weights.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
225	            # Samples not selected by any expert fall back to softmax (very
226	            # rare when capacity_factor>=1).
227	            weights = torch.where(
228	                denom > 1e-8,
229	                weights / denom.clamp_min(1e-8),
230	                S,
231	            )
232	            return weights
233	
234	        raise ValueError("Unknown router_type: %s" % self.router_type)
235	
236	    def forward(self, hidden_states, raw_input=None):
237	        self._last_raw_input = raw_input  # cache for aux losses
238	        logits = self._compute_logits(hidden_states, raw_input)
239	        weights = self._compute_weights(logits)  # (B, K)
240	
241	        outputs = torch.stack([a(hidden_states) for a in self.adapters], dim=1)  # (B, K, H)
242	        mixed = (weights.unsqueeze(-1) * outputs).sum(dim=1)  # (B, H)
243	        return mixed
244	
245	    def get_routing_stats(self, hidden_states, raw_input=None):
246	        """Return (B, K) mixture weights for analysis (no grad)."""
247	        with torch.no_grad():
248	            logits = self._compute_logits(hidden_states, raw_input)
249	            weights = self._compute_weights(logits)
250	        return weights
251	
252	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
253	    #  Auxiliary loss terms (all return scalars, pre-coefficient)        #
254	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
255	
256	    def load_balance_loss(self, hidden_states):
257	        """Switch Transformer auxiliary loss (Fedus et al. 2022, Eq. 4-6).
258	
259	        L_B = N * Σ_i f_i * P_i
260	
261	        - load_balance_variant == "mean-prob": legacy formulation where f_i = P_i
262	          (equivalent to N * Σ P_i^2).  Preserved so prior Table 2 numbers in
263	          the paper reproduce exactly.
264	        - load_balance_variant == "argmax":    correct Switch Transformer form
265	          where f_i uses the non-differentiable argmax indicator while P_i
266	          remains the differentiable mean probability.
267	        """
268	        raw = getattr(self, '_last_raw_input', None)
269	        logits = self._compute_logits(hidden_states, raw)
270	        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)          # (B, K)
271	        P_i = probs.mean(dim=0)                    # (K,)
272	        if self.load_balance_variant == "argmax":
273	            argmax_idx = probs.argmax(dim=-1)      # (B,)
274	            f_i = F.one_hot(argmax_idx, self.K).float().mean(dim=0)  # (K,)
275	        else:
276	            f_i = P_i
277	        return self.K * (f_i * P_i).sum()
278	
279	    def entropy_regularization(self, hidden_states):
280	        """Negative entropy of the router distribution: −H(p).
281	
282	        Added with a POSITIVE coefficient, this encourages HIGH routing
283	        entropy (forces diversity).  Returning −H rather than +H means the
284	        training-loop combination line stays the simple pattern
285	            loss = mse + coef * term.
286	        """
287	        raw = getattr(self, '_last_raw_input', None)
288	        logits = self._compute_logits(hidden_states, raw)
289	        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
290	        H = -(probs * torch.log(probs.clamp_min(1e-10))).sum(dim=-1).mean()
291	        return -H
292	
293	    def z_loss(self, hidden_states):
294	        """ST-MoE router z-loss (Zoph et al. 2022, Eq. 5):
295	            L_z = (1/B) * Σ_i (log Σ_j exp(x_j^(i)))^2
296	
297	        Penalizes large router logit magnitudes; empirically stabilizes
298	        bfloat16 training and the routing distribution."""
299	        raw = getattr(self, '_last_raw_input', None)
300	        logits = self._compute_logits(hidden_states, raw)
301	        lse = torch.logsumexp(logits, dim=-1)      # (B,)
302	        return (lse ** 2).mean()
303	
304	    def relu_l1_loss(self, hidden_states):
305	        """ReMoE L1 sparsity regularization (Wang et al. 2025, Eq. 9).
306	
307	        Only meaningful when router_type == 'relu'; we return 0 otherwise so
308	        the training loop can unconditionally add `relu_l1_coef * relu_l1_loss()`.
309	        Uses the load-balanced variant (Eq. 10) which weights over-active
310	        experts more aggressively, identical to Switch load balancing up to a
311	        constant once experts are used."""
312	        if self.router_type != "relu":
313	            return hidden_states.new_zeros(())
314	        raw = getattr(self, '_last_raw_input', None)
315	        logits = self._compute_logits(hidden_states, raw)
316	        raw = F.relu(logits)                       # (B, K)
317	        # Non-differentiable f_{l,e} = (K / k*B) * count(raw > 0) [k=1 default].
318	        active = (raw > 0).float()
319	        f_e = active.mean(dim=0) * self.K           # (K,) (k/E normalization)
320	        # Avoid zero gradient when everyone is firing
321	        weight = f_e.detach().clamp_min(1.0)
322	        return (weight * raw.mean(dim=0)).sum()
323	
324	    def total_aux_loss(self, hidden_states):
325	        """Sum of all enabled auxiliary losses, each pre-multiplied by its
326	        coefficient.  Called from the training loop as a single line."""
327	        total = hidden_states.new_zeros(())
328	        if self.load_balance_coef != 0.0:
329	            total = total + self.load_balance_coef * self.load_balance_loss(hidden_states)
330	        if self.entropy_reg_coef != 0.0:
331	            total = total + self.entropy_reg_coef * self.entropy_regularization(hidden_states)
332	        if self.z_loss_coef != 0.0:
333	            total = total + self.z_loss_coef * self.z_loss(hidden_states)
334	        if self.relu_l1_coef != 0.0 and self.router_type == "relu":
335	            total = total + self.relu_l1_coef * self.relu_l1_loss(hidden_states)
336	        return total
337	
338	    def param_count(self):
339	        return sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters())
340	
341	
342	def train_adamix(model, blocks, X_train, Y_train, X_val, Y_val, X_test, Y_test,
343	                 device="cuda", n_epochs=15, forecast_horizon=96, batch_size=128,
344	                 backbone_type="moment", K=5, hidden=64, test_ch=None, scaler=None,
345	                 trajectory_path=None, trajectory_max_steps=400,
346	                 router_type="softmax",
347	                 load_balance_coef=0.01,
348	                 load_balance_variant="mean-prob",
349	                 entropy_reg_coef=0.0,
350	                 z_loss_coef=0.0,
351	                 relu_l1_coef=0.0,
352	                 capacity_factor=2.0,
353	                 router_input="hidden"):
354	    """Train AdaMix adapter.
355	
356	    If ``trajectory_path`` is not None, writes a JSONL file with per-step
357	    routing / gradient diagnostics for the first ``trajectory_max_steps``
358	    optimizer steps. Used by T2.A to provide mechanistic evidence for the
359	    gradient co-adaptation hypothesis (see review W5): under an unfrozen
360	    backbone, we expect to see (a) the router entropy collapse toward 0 within
361	    a few dozen steps, (b) one expert's gradient norm grow relative to the
362	    others, and (c) the backbone encoder-block gradient norm remain active
363	    (confirming joint updates to $theta_active$). Under the frozen control,
364	    (a)--(c) should all be absent.
365	    """
366	    hdim = _get_hidden_dim(model)
367	    adapter = AdaMix(
368	        hdim, forecast_horizon, K=K, hidden=hidden,
369	        router_type=router_type,
370	        load_balance_coef=load_balance_coef,
371	        load_balance_variant=load_balance_variant,
372	        entropy_reg_coef=entropy_reg_coef,
373	        z_loss_coef=z_loss_coef,
374	        relu_l1_coef=relu_l1_coef,
375	        capacity_factor=capacity_factor,
376	        router_input=router_input,
377	    ).to(device)
378	
379	    # Collect trainable params (adapter + unfrozen backbone)
380	    trainable = list(adapter.parameters())
381	    pids = {id(p) for p in trainable}
382	    for p in model.parameters():
383	        if p.requires_grad and id(p) not in pids:
384	            trainable.append(p)
385	            pids.add(id(p))
386	
387	    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(trainable, lr=1e-3)
388	    mse_fn = nn.MSELoss()
389	    use_amp = device == "cuda"
390	
391	    train_loader = DataLoader(TensorDataset(
392	        torch.from_numpy(X_train).float(), torch.from_numpy(Y_train).float(),
393	    ), batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True)
394	
395	    val_loader = DataLoader(TensorDataset(
396	        torch.from_numpy(X_val).float(), torch.from_numpy(Y_val).float(),
397	    ), batch_size=batch_size)
398	
399	    # T2.A trajectory logging: open file and take a snapshot of the
400	    # unfrozen encoder-block parameters for grad-norm tracking.
401	    trajectory_file = None
402	    if trajectory_path is not None:
403	        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(trajectory_path) or ".", exist_ok=True)
404	        trajectory_file = open(trajectory_path, "w")
405	    unfrozen_block_params = [p for b in blocks for p in b.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
406	    step_counter = [0]  # mutable closure
407	
408	    for epoch in range(n_epochs):
409	        model.train(); adapter.train()
410	        for bx, by in train_loader:
411	            raw_flat = bx.to(device)  # (B, L) — raw input before unsqueeze
412	            bx, by = raw_flat.unsqueeze(1), by.to(device)
413	            mask = torch.ones(bx.shape[0], bx.shape[2], device=device)
414	            with torch.amp.autocast('cuda', dtype=torch.bfloat16, enabled=use_amp):
415	                feat = _extract_features_batch(model, blocks, bx, mask, backbone_type=backbone_type)
416	                raw_for_router = raw_flat if router_input == "raw" else None
417	                pred = adapter(feat, raw_input=raw_for_router)
418	                loss = mse_fn(pred, by) + adapter.total_aux_loss(feat)
419	            optimizer.zero_grad()
420	            loss.backward()
421	
422	            # T2.A: snapshot gradient / routing state BEFORE optimizer.step().
423	            if trajectory_file is not None and step_counter[0] < trajectory_max_steps:
424	                with torch.no_grad():
425	                    h_summary = feat.mean(dim=1)
426	                    logits = adapter.router(h_summary)
427	                    weights = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1).float()
428	                    mean_weights = weights.mean(dim=0)
429	                    entropy = -(weights * torch.log(weights + 1e-10)).sum(dim=-1).mean().item()
430	                    max_w = weights.max(dim=-1).values.mean().item()
431	
432	                    expert_grad_norms = []
433	                    for e_idx, expert in enumerate(adapter.adapters):
434	                        g_sq = 0.0
435	                        for p in expert.parameters():
436	                            if p.grad is not None:
437	                                g_sq += float(p.grad.detach().float().pow(2).sum().item())
438	                        expert_grad_norms.append(g_sq ** 0.5)
439	
440	                    router_grad_sq = 0.0
441	                    for p in adapter.router.parameters():
442	                        if p.grad is not None:
443	                            router_grad_sq += float(p.grad.detach().float().pow(2).sum().item())
444	                    router_grad_norm = router_grad_sq ** 0.5
445	
446	                    backbone_grad_sq = 0.0
447	                    for p in unfrozen_block_params:
448	                        if p.grad is not None:
449	                            backbone_grad_sq += float(p.grad.detach().float().pow(2).sum().item())
450	                    backbone_grad_norm = backbone_grad_sq ** 0.5
451	
452	                    trajectory_file.write(json.dumps({
453	                        "step": step_counter[0],
454	                        "epoch": epoch,
455	                        "loss": float(loss.item()),
456	                        "routing_entropy": float(entropy),
457	                        "routing_max_weight": float(max_w),
458	                        "mean_routing_weights": [float(w) for w in mean_weights.tolist()],
459	                        "expert_grad_norms": expert_grad_norms,
460	                        "router_grad_norm": router_grad_norm,
461	                        "backbone_unfrozen_grad_norm": backbone_grad_norm,
462	                    }) + "\n")
463	                    trajectory_file.flush()
464	                step_counter[0] += 1
465	
466	            optimizer.step()
467	
468	    if trajectory_file is not None:
469	        trajectory_file.close()
470	        print("Trajectory saved: %s (%d steps)" % (trajectory_path, step_counter[0]))
471	
472	    # Evaluate on test
473	    model.eval(); adapter.eval()
474	    test_loader = DataLoader(TensorDataset(
475	        torch.from_numpy(X_test).float(), torch.from_numpy(Y_test).float(),
476	    ), batch_size=batch_size)
477	
478	    preds, tgts = [], []
479	    all_routing_weights = []
480	    with torch.no_grad(), torch.amp.autocast('cuda', dtype=torch.bfloat16, enabled=use_amp):
481	        for bx, by in test_loader:
482	            raw_flat = bx.to(device)
483	            bx, by = raw_flat.unsqueeze(1), by.to(device)
484	            mask = torch.ones(bx.shape[0], bx.shape[2], device=device)
485	            feat = _extract_features_batch(model, blocks, bx, mask, backbone_type=backbone_type)
486	            raw_for_router = raw_flat if router_input == "raw" else None
487	            preds.append(adapter(feat, raw_input=raw_for_router).float().cpu())
488	            tgts.append(by.cpu())
489	            all_routing_weights.append(adapter.get_routing_stats(feat, raw_input=raw_for_router).cpu())
490	
491	    preds, tgts = torch.cat(preds), torch.cat(tgts)
492	    routing = torch.cat(all_routing_weights)
493	
494	    mse = nn.MSELoss()(preds, tgts).item()
495	    mae = nn.L1Loss()(preds, tgts).item()
496	
497	    # Routing analysis
498	    mean_routing = routing.mean(dim=0).tolist()
499	    routing_entropy = -(routing * torch.log(routing + 1e-10)).sum(dim=-1).mean().item()
500	
501	    out = {
502	        "mse": mse,
503	        "mae": mae,
504	        "param_count": adapter.param_count(),
505	        "mean_routing_weights": {HEAD_NAMES[i]: round(w, 3) for i, w in enumerate(mean_routing[:len(HEAD_NAMES)])},
506	        "routing_entropy": routing_entropy,
507	    }
508	    if test_ch is not None and scaler is not None:
509	        mse_d, mae_d = compute_denorm_mse(preds, tgts, test_ch, scaler)
510	        out["mse_denorm"] = mse_d
511	        out["mae_denorm"] = mae_d
512	    return out
513	
514	
515	def _apply_unfreeze(blocks, unfreeze):
516	    """Selectively unfreeze encoder blocks."""
517	    n = len(blocks)
518	    if unfreeze == "frozen":
519	        return
520	    elif unfreeze == "last2":
521	        start = max(0, n - 2)
522	    elif unfreeze == "last4":
523	        start = max(0, n - 4)
524	    elif unfreeze == "all":
525	        start = 0
526	    else:
527	        raise ValueError("Unknown unfreeze: %s" % unfreeze)
528	    for i in range(start, n):
529	        for p in blocks[i].parameters():
530	            p.requires_grad = True
531	
532	
533	def main():
534	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
535	    parser.add_argument("--dataset", default="ETTh1")
536	    parser.add_argument("--horizon", type=int, default=96)
537	    parser.add_argument("--K", type=int, default=5)
538	    parser.add_argument("--hidden", type=int, default=64)
539	    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=15)
540	    parser.add_argument("--unfreeze", default="last4", choices=["frozen", "last2", "last4", "all"],
541	                        help="Backbone unfreezing strategy")
542	    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
543	    parser.add_argument("--backbone", default="AutonLab/MOMENT-1-small")
544	    parser.add_argument("--device", default="cuda")
545	    parser.add_argument("--trajectory", default=None,
546	                        help="Path to write per-step routing/gradient trajectory JSONL "
547	                             "(T2.A mechanistic verification). If omitted, no trajectory is "
548	                             "recorded. Example: --trajectory results/adamix/trajectory_ETTh1_last4_42.jsonl")
549	    parser.add_argument("--trajectory-max-steps", type=int, default=400)
550	    parser.add_argument("--disable-revin", action="store_true",
551	                        help="Controlled ablation: disable RevIN inside MOMENT backbone")
552	    parser.add_argument("--norm-type", default="revin",
553	                        choices=["revin", "batchnorm", "groupnorm"],
554	                        help="Normalization type inside MOMENT (generalization test)")
555	    # --- MoE rescue-baseline flags (Section 3 rescue sub-table) -----------
556	    parser.add_argument("--router-type", default="softmax",
557	                        choices=["softmax", "relu", "expert-choice"],
558	                        help="Routing mechanism. softmax=Switch Transformer "
559	                             "(default/legacy), relu=ReMoE ReLU gate (Wang et "
560	                             "al. ICLR 2025), expert-choice=Zhou et al. 2022 "
561	                             "per-expert top-k.")
562	    parser.add_argument("--load-balance-coef", type=float, default=0.01,
563	                        help="Coefficient on Switch Transformer load-balance "
564	                             "auxiliary loss (alpha in Fedus et al. Eq. 4). "
565	                             "Default 0.01 matches paper Table 2.")
566	    parser.add_argument("--load-balance-variant", default="mean-prob",
567	                        choices=["mean-prob", "argmax"],
568	                        help="mean-prob: legacy AdaMix form f_i=P_i (reproduces "
569	                             "existing paper numbers). argmax: correct Switch "
570	                             "Transformer form where f_i is the argmax indicator.")
571	    parser.add_argument("--entropy-reg-coef", type=float, default=0.0,
572	                        help="Coefficient on -H(router) regularizer. Positive "
573	                             "values encourage uniform routing. Rescue sweep: "
574	                             "{0, 0.01, 0.1, 1.0}.")
575	    parser.add_argument("--z-loss-coef", type=float, default=0.0,
576	                        help="Coefficient on ST-MoE router z-loss (Zoph et al. "
577	                             "2022 Eq. 5). Recommended 0.001; rescue sweep "
578	                             "{0, 0.001, 0.01, 0.1}.")
579	    parser.add_argument("--relu-l1-coef", type=float, default=0.0,
580	                        help="Coefficient on ReMoE L1 sparsity regularizer. "
581	                             "Only used when --router-type=relu. Rescue sweep "
582	                             "uses fixed value {0.001, 0.01, 0.1}.")
583	    parser.add_argument("--capacity-factor", type=float, default=2.0,
584	                        help="Expert-choice routing capacity factor c. "
585	                             "k = B*c/K tokens per expert (Zhou et al. 2022).")
586	    parser.add_argument("--router-input", default="hidden",
587	                        choices=["hidden", "raw"],
588	                        help="Router input source. hidden=mean-pooled hidden states "
589	                             "(default AdaMix). raw=pre-normalization raw input "
590	                             "(Exp D: proves diagnosis is architecture-agnostic).")
591	    parser.add_argument("--results-dir", default="results/adamix",
592	                        help="Output directory. Rescue sweep writes to "
593	                             "results/adamix_rescue/.")
594	    parser.add_argument("--run-baselines", default="auto",
595	                        choices=["auto", "yes", "no"],
596	                        help="Whether to also run fixed-head baselines. "
597	                             "'auto' skips baselines when rescue flags are set "
598	                             "(to keep the rescue sweep cheap), runs them "
599	                             "otherwise.")
600	    args = parser.parse_args()
601	
602	    # Detect whether we're running a rescue-sweep config (any non-default
603	    # rescue flag set).  Default baseline configurations are written to
604	    # results/adamix/ to avoid churning existing experiments; rescue configs
605	    # go to results/adamix_rescue/ for easy analysis.
606	    rescue_active = (
607	        args.router_type != "softmax"
608	        or args.entropy_reg_coef != 0.0
609	        or args.z_loss_coef != 0.0
610	        or args.relu_l1_coef != 0.0
611	        or args.load_balance_variant != "mean-prob"
612	        or args.load_balance_coef != 0.01
613	    )
614	    results_dir = args.results_dir
615	    if rescue_active and results_dir == "results/adamix":
616	        results_dir = "results/adamix_rescue"
617	
618	    os.makedirs(results_dir, exist_ok=True)
619	    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
620	    np.random.seed(args.seed)
621	
622	    # Load model
623	    print("Loading %s..." % args.backbone)
624	    model = load_backbone(args.backbone, args.device,
625	                          disable_revin=args.disable_revin,
626	                          norm_type=args.norm_type)
627	    _disable_gradient_checkpointing(model)
628	    blocks = _get_encoder_blocks(model)
629	    hdim = _get_hidden_dim(model)
630	    bb_type = _detect_backbone_type(args.backbone)
631	
632	    for p in model.parameters():
633	        p.requires_grad = False
634	    _apply_unfreeze(blocks, args.unfreeze)
635	    backbone_trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
636	    print("d_model=%d, K=%d, hidden=%d, unfreeze=%s, backbone_trainable=%d" % (
637	        hdim, args.K, args.hidden, args.unfreeze, backbone_trainable))
638	
639	    # Load data
640	    splits, _ = load_standard_data(args.dataset, args.horizon)
641	    X_train, Y_train = splits["train"]
642	    X_val, Y_val = splits["val"]
643	    X_test, Y_test = splits["test"]
644	    test_ch = splits.get("test_ch")
645	    scaler = splits.get("_scaler")
646	    print("%s H=%d: train=%d, val=%d, test=%d" % (
647	        args.dataset, args.horizon, len(X_train), len(X_val), len(X_test)))
648	
649	    # === Run AdaMix ===
650	    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
651	    print("AdaMix: %s H=%d K=%d seed=%d" % (args.dataset, args.horizon, args.K, args.seed))
652	    print("=" * 60)
653	
654	    start = time.time()
655	    result = train_adamix(
656	        model, blocks, X_train, Y_train, X_val, Y_val, X_test, Y_test,
657	        device=args.device, n_epochs=args.epochs, forecast_horizon=args.horizon,
658	        backbone_type=bb_type, K=args.K, hidden=args.hidden,
659	        test_ch=test_ch, scaler=scaler,
660	        trajectory_path=args.trajectory,
661	        trajectory_max_steps=args.trajectory_max_steps,
662	        router_type=args.router_type,
663	        load_balance_coef=args.load_balance_coef,
664	        load_balance_variant=args.load_balance_variant,
665	        entropy_reg_coef=args.entropy_reg_coef,
666	        z_loss_coef=args.z_loss_coef,
667	        relu_l1_coef=args.relu_l1_coef,
668	        capacity_factor=args.capacity_factor,
669	        router_input=args.router_input,
670	    )
671	    elapsed = time.time() - start
672	
673	    print("AdaMix: MSE=%.4f  MAE=%.4f  params=%d  time=%.0fs" % (
674	        result["mse"], result["mae"], result["param_count"], elapsed))
675	    if "mse_denorm" in result:
676	        print("AdaMix: MSE_denorm=%.4f  MAE_denorm=%.4f (original units)" % (
677	            result["mse_denorm"], result["mae_denorm"]))
678	    print("Routing: %s" % result["mean_routing_weights"])
679	    print("Routing entropy: %.3f (max=%.3f for K=%d)" % (
680	        result["routing_entropy"], np.log(args.K), args.K))
681	
682	    # === Run fixed baselines (skipped during rescue sweeps) ===
683	    run_baselines = (args.run_baselines == "yes" or
684	                     (args.run_baselines == "auto" and not rescue_active))
685	    baseline_results = {}
686	    if run_baselines:
687	        print("\nFixed baselines (%d epochs, unfreeze=%s):" % (args.epochs, args.unfreeze))
688	        # Reload model for fair baseline comparison
689	        model2 = load_backbone(args.backbone, args.device,
690	                               disable_revin=args.disable_revin)
691	        _disable_gradient_checkpointing(model2)
692	        blocks2 = _get_encoder_blocks(model2)
693	        for p in model2.parameters():
694	            p.requires_grad = False
695	        _apply_unfreeze(blocks2, args.unfreeze)
696	
697	        baselines = {"linear": SEED_ADAPTERS[0], "attention": SEED_ADAPTERS[3], "conv": SEED_ADAPTERS[4]}
698	        for name, code in baselines.items():
699	            try:
700	                tr = train_adapter(code, model2, blocks2, X_train, Y_train, X_test, Y_test,
701	                                   device=args.device, n_epochs=15, forecast_horizon=args.horizon,
702	                                   backbone_type=bb_type, eval_ch=test_ch, scaler=scaler)
703	                baseline_results[name] = tr
704	                if "mse_denorm" in tr:
705	                    print("  %-15s MSE=%.4f  MSE_denorm=%.4f  params=%d" % (
706	                        name, tr["mse"], tr["mse_denorm"], tr["param_count"]))
707	                else:
708	                    print("  %-15s MSE=%.4f  params=%d" % (name, tr["mse"], tr["param_count"]))
709	            except Exception as e:
710	                print("  %-15s ERROR: %s" % (name, e))
711	    else:
712	        print("\nFixed baselines SKIPPED (rescue sweep mode)")
713	
714	    # Summary
715	    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
716	    print("SUMMARY: %s H=%d" % (args.dataset, args.horizon))
717	    print("=" * 60)
718	    print("AdaMix (K=%d):  MSE=%.4f  params=%d  time=%.0fs" % (
719	        args.K, result["mse"], result["param_count"], elapsed))
720	    if baseline_results:
721	        best_bl_name = min(baseline_results, key=lambda k: baseline_results[k]["mse"])
722	        best_bl_mse = baseline_results[best_bl_name]["mse"]
723	        delta = (result["mse"] - best_bl_mse) / best_bl_mse * 100
724	        winner = "AdaMix" if result["mse"] < best_bl_mse else "BASELINE"
725	        print("Best baseline: MSE=%.4f  (%s)" % (best_bl_mse, best_bl_name))
726	        print("Delta: %+.1f%% -> Winner: %s" % (delta, winner))
727	    else:
728	        delta = None
729	        winner = None
730	
731	    # Save
732	    scaler_info = None
733	    if scaler is not None:
734	        scaler_info = {
735	            "scale_": [float(x) for x in scaler.scale_],
736	            "mean_": [float(x) for x in scaler.mean_],
737	            "mean_scale_sq": float(np.mean(scaler.scale_ ** 2)),
738	        }
739	    save_data = {
740	        "dataset": args.dataset,
741	        "horizon": args.horizon,
742	        "seed": args.seed,
743	        "K": args.K,
744	        "hidden": args.hidden,
745	        "unfreeze": args.unfreeze,
746	        "disable_revin": args.disable_revin,
747	        "backbone_trainable_params": backbone_trainable,
748	        "adamix": result,
749	        "elapsed": elapsed,
750	        "baselines": {k: v for k, v in baseline_results.items()},
751	        "winner": winner,
752	        "delta_pct": delta,
753	        "scaler": scaler_info,
754	        # Rescue-baseline configuration (present on every run so the summary
755	        # scripts can slice by these fields):
756	        "router_type": args.router_type,
757	        "load_balance_coef": args.load_balance_coef,
758	        "load_balance_variant": args.load_balance_variant,
759	        "entropy_reg_coef": args.entropy_reg_coef,
760	        "z_loss_coef": args.z_loss_coef,
761	        "relu_l1_coef": args.relu_l1_coef,
762	        "capacity_factor": args.capacity_factor,
763	        "rescue_active": rescue_active,
764	    }
765	    revin_suffix = "_no_revin" if args.disable_revin else ""
766	    norm_suffix = "_%s" % args.norm_type if args.norm_type != "revin" else ""
767	    raw_suffix = "_rawrouter" if args.router_input == "raw" else ""
768	    # Encode the rescue configuration in the filename so concurrent sweeps
769	    # never collide on the same output file.
770	    if rescue_active:
771	        rescue_tag = "_rtr%s_lb%g_lv%s_ent%g_z%g_l1%g_cf%g" % (
772	            args.router_type, args.load_balance_coef, args.load_balance_variant,
